内容:
HSBC 开始覆盖 SpaceX (SPCX)，给予「持有」评级，目标价 $115。

Why it matters:
Sell-side coverage from a bulge-bracket bank signals SPCX is being treated as a liquid, trackable equity rather than a private curiosity — a precondition for index inclusion and passive-flow exposure once shares trade publicly. A Hold rather than Buy/Sell also suggests HSBC sees the stock as roughly fairly valued at current levels, tempering the pure momentum narrative.

影响:
一家大型投行开始覆盖 SPCX，说明市场开始把它当作一只可追踪的流动性股票，而不只是一家私人公司——这是未来被纳入指数、获得被动资金流入的前提条件。评级是「持有」而非「买入」或「卖出」，也说明 HSBC 认为当前股价大致合理，为纯粹的动能叙事降温。

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.39% (+56bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-23 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-22
Current:  2.39%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +56bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
A SpaceX (SPCX) lock-up tranche is estimated to expire within the next week, releasing a block of insider shares into the float.

Why it matters:
SPCX floated ~4% of its shares and was fast-tracked into the Nasdaq-100, so index funds were forced to buy into a tiny float. Each unlock expands supply against that fixed passive bid; the large tranches (Q3'26 earnings ≈13亿, Day-366 Musk tranche ≈64亿) are the live test of whether index plumbing can absorb mega-IPO distribution — the same mechanics an OpenAI/Anthropic listing would face. Earnings-linked dates are estimates; confirm on EDGAR.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.37% (+54bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-22 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-21
Current:  2.37%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +54bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.35% (+52bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-21 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-20
Current:  2.35%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +52bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.31% (+48bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-20 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-17
Current:  2.31%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +48bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.
